\displaystyle(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}}+\tfrac{\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}-(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I+(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\tfrac{\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}}}}\right)^{-1}-I}}\right)(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}.

By Corollary
26
, for sufficiently large
n
,
\|(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\frac{\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}}\|_{2}<1
with probability of at least
1-\delta
.
Hence

\displaystyle(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}-(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I+(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\tfrac{\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}}}}\right)^{-1}-I}}\right)(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\tfrac{\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}}}}\right)^{j}(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}.

∎

Lemma 29
.

Assume that
\mu_{0}=0
and
\sigma^{2}=\Theta(n^{t})
where
1-\frac{\alpha}{1+2\tau}<t<1
. Let
R=n^{(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)(1-t)}
where
0<\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau+(1+2\tau)t}{\alpha^{2}(1-t)}
. Then when
n
is sufficiently large, with probability of at least
1-2\delta
we have

\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\cdot n^{\max\{-(1-t),\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{2\alpha}\}}}}\right).

(54)

Proof of Lemma
29
.

Let
\Lambda_{1:R}=\mathrm{diag}\{\lambda_{1},\ldots,\lambda_{R}\}
,
\Phi_{1:R}=(\phi_{1}(\mathbf{x}),\phi_{1}(\mathbf{x}),\ldots,\phi_{R}(\mathbf{x}))
and
\bm{\mu}_{1:R}=(\mu_{1},\ldots,\mu_{R})
. Since
\mu_{0}=0
, we have
(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})=(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{1:R}\Lambda_{1:R}\Phi_{1:R}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{1:R}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}
.
Using the Woodbury matrix identity, we have that

\displaystyle(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{1:R}\Lambda_{1:R}\Phi_{1:R}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{1:R}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}=

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[I-\Phi_{1:R}(\sigma^{2}I+\Lambda_{1:R}\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R})^{-1}\Lambda_{1:R}\Phi_{1:R}^{T}}}\right]\Phi_{1:R}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}

(55)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{1:R}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}-\Phi_{1:R}(\sigma^{2}I+\Lambda_{1:R}\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R})^{-1}\Lambda_{1:R}\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{1:R}(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R}\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}.

Let
A=(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1/2}\Lambda_{1:R}^{1/2}(\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R}-nI)\Lambda_{1:R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1/2}
.By Corollary
22
, with probability of at least
1-\delta
, we have
\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}=\sqrt{\log\frac{R}{\delta}}n^{\frac{1-\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}-\frac{(1+2\tau)t}{2\alpha}}
.
When
n
is sufficiently large,
\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}=o(1)
is less than
1
because
1-\frac{\alpha}{1+2\tau}<t<1
. By Lemma
27
, we have

\displaystyle(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R}\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R})^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\Lambda_{1:R}(\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R}-nI)}}\right)^{j}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}.

We then have

\displaystyle\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R}\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2}

(56)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\Lambda_{1:R}(\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R}-nI)}}\right)^{j}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}}}\right)\bm{\mu}_{1:R}}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\Lambda_{1:R}(\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R}-nI)}}\right)^{j}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}}}\right\|_{2}}}\right).

By Lemma
15
and Assumption
5
, assuming that
\sup_{i\geq 1}p_{i+1}-p_{i}=h
, we have

\displaystyle\|(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\sum_{p=1}^{R}\frac{C_{\mu}^{2}p^{-2\beta}}{(1+n\underline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}/\sigma^{2})^{2}}}=\Theta(n^{\max\{-(1-t),\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{2\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n),

\displaystyle\|(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\sqrt{\sum_{i=1}^{\lfloor\frac{R}{h}\rfloor}\frac{\underline{C_{\mu}^{2}}i^{-2\beta}}{(1+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\overline{C_{\lambda}}(hi)^{-\alpha})^{2}}}=\Theta(n^{\max\{-(1-t),\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{2\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)

where
k=\begin{cases}0,&2\alpha\not=2\beta-1,\\
1,&2\alpha=2\beta-1.\end{cases}
.
Overall we have

\|(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2}=\Theta(n^{(1-t)\max\{-1,\tfrac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n).

(57)

Using the fact that
\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}=\sqrt{\log\frac{R}{\delta}}n^{\frac{1-\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}-\frac{(1+2\tau)t}{2\alpha}}
and
\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\Lambda_{1:R}\|_{2}\leq n^{-1}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\Lambda_{1:R}(\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R}-nI)}}\right)^{j}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}}}\right\|_{2}

(58)

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-\tfrac{1}{2}}\Lambda_{1:R}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A)^{j}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-\tfrac{1}{2}}\Lambda_{1:R}^{\tfrac{1}{2}}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{O}(n^{-\frac{1-t}{2}})\|\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}^{j}\|(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-\tfrac{1}{2}}\Lambda_{1:R}^{-\tfrac{1}{2}}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2}

By Lemma
16
and the assumption
R=n^{(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)(1-t)}
,

\displaystyle\|(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-\frac{1}{2}}\Lambda_{1:R}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{p=1}^{R}\frac{(\underline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha})^{-1}C_{\mu}^{2}p^{-2\beta}}{(1+n\underline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}/\sigma^{2})^{1}}}

(59)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}(\max\{n^{-(1-t)/2},R^{1/2-\beta+\alpha/2}\})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}(\max\{n^{-(1-t)/2},n^{(\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}+\kappa(1/2-\beta+\alpha/2))(1-t)}\})

We then have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\Lambda_{1:R}(\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R}-nI)}}\right)^{j}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}}}\right\|_{2}

(60)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\|\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}^{j}\tilde{O}(\max\{n^{-(1-t)},n^{(\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}+\kappa(1/2-\beta+\alpha/2))(1-t)}\})

By (
56
), (
57
) and (
60
), we have

\displaystyle\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R}\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2}

(61)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Theta(n^{(1-t)\max\{-1,\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}^{j}\tilde{O}(\max\{n^{-(1-t)},n^{(1-t)\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}+\kappa(1-t)(1/2-\beta+\alpha/2)}\})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Theta(n^{(1-t)\max\{-1,\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)+\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{1-\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}-\frac{(1+2\tau)t}{2\alpha}})\tilde{O}(\max\{n^{-(1-t)},n^{(1-t)\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}+\kappa(1-t)(1/2-\beta+\alpha/2)}\}).

By assumption
\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau+(1+2\tau)t}{\alpha^{2}(1-t)}
, we have that

\displaystyle\kappa(1-t)(1/2-\beta+\alpha/2)+\frac{1-\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}-\frac{(1+2\tau)t}{2\alpha}<\kappa\alpha(1-t)/2+\frac{1-\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}-\frac{(1+2\tau)t}{2\alpha}<0.

Using (
61
), we then get

\displaystyle\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R}\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta(n^{(1-t)\max\{-1,\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)

(62)

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{\sigma^{2}}\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2}.

By Corollary
20
, with probability of at least
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\|\Phi_{1:R}(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R}\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2}=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}(\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R}\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2})

(63)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}(\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\cdot n^{(1-t)\max\{-1,\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}).

From (
55
), we get
\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{1:R}\Lambda_{1:R}\Phi_{1:R}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{1:R}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2}=\tilde{O}(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\cdot n^{(1-t)\max\{-1,\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}})
.
This concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma 30
.

Assume that
\mu_{0}>0
and
\sigma^{2}=\Theta(n^{t})
where
1-\frac{\alpha}{1+2\tau}<t<1
. Let
R=n^{\tfrac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa}
where
0<\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau+(1+2\tau)t}{\alpha^{2}}
. Then when
n
is sufficiently large, with probability of at least
1-2\delta
, we have

\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n}}}\right).

(64)

Proof of Lemma
30
.

Using the Woodbury matrix identity, we have that

\displaystyle(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})=

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[I-\Phi_{R}(\sigma^{2}I+\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}}\right]\Phi_{R}\bm{\mu}_{R}

(65)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{R}\bm{\mu}_{R}-\Phi_{R}(\sigma^{2}I+\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}\bm{\mu}_{R}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{R}(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}.

Let
\bm{\mu}_{R,1}=(\mu_{0},0,\ldots,0)
and
\bm{\mu}_{R,2}=(0,\mu_{1},\ldots,\mu_{R})
. Then
\bm{\mu}_{R}=\bm{\mu}_{R,1}+\bm{\mu}_{R,2}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}\|_{2}=\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R,1}\|_{2}+\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R,2}\|_{2}.

(66)

According to (
62
) in the proof of Lemma
29
, we have
\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R,2}\|_{2}=\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{-(1-t),\frac{(1-t)(1-2\beta)}{2\alpha}\}})
. Next we estimate
\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R,1}\|_{2}
.

Let

A=(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-\gamma/2}\Lambda_{1:R}^{\gamma/2}(\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R}-nI)\Lambda_{1:R}^{\gamma/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-\gamma/2}

where
\frac{1}{1-t}(\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}-\frac{(1+2\tau+2\alpha)t}{2\alpha})<\gamma<1
. Since
1-\frac{\alpha}{1+2\tau}<t<1
,
\frac{1}{1-t}(\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}-\frac{(1+2\tau+2\alpha)t}{2\alpha})<1
so the range for
\gamma
is well-defined.By Corollary
22
, with probability of at least
1-\delta
, we have
\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}=\tilde{O}(\sqrt{\log\frac{R}{\delta}}n^{\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}-\frac{(1+2\tau+2\alpha)t}{2\alpha}-\gamma(1-t)})=o(1)
.
When
n
is sufficiently large,
\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}
is less than
1
because
1-\frac{\alpha}{1+2\tau}<t<1
. By Lemma
27
, we have

\displaystyle(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)}}\right)^{j}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}.

We then have

\displaystyle\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R,1}\|_{2}

(67)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)}}\right)^{j}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}}}\right)\bm{\mu}_{R,1}}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R,1}\|_{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)}}\right)^{j}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R,1}}}\right\|_{2}}}\right).

By Lemma
15
,

\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R,1}\|_{2}\leq\sqrt{\mu_{0}^{2}+\sum_{p=1}^{R}\frac{C_{\mu}^{2}p^{-2\beta}}{(1+n\underline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}/\sigma^{2})^{2}}}=O(1).

(68)

Let
\tilde{\Lambda}_{1,R}=\mathrm{diag}\{1,\lambda_{1},\ldots,\lambda_{R}\}
and
I_{0,R}=(0,1,\ldots,1)
.
Then
\Lambda_{R}=\tilde{\Lambda}_{1,R}I_{0,R}
.
Let
B=(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-\gamma/2}\tilde{\Lambda}_{1,R}^{\gamma/2}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)\tilde{\Lambda}_{1,R}^{\gamma/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-\gamma/2}
. According to Corollary
23
, we have
\|B\|_{2}=O\big(\sqrt{\log\frac{R}{\delta}}n^{\frac{1}{2}}\big)
.
Using the fact that
\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}=\tilde{O}\big(\sqrt{\log\frac{R}{\delta}}n^{\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}-\frac{(1+2\tau+2\alpha)t}{2\alpha}-\gamma(1-t)}\big)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)}}\right)^{j}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R,1}}}\right\|_{2}

(69)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1+\tfrac{\gamma}{2}}\Lambda_{R}^{1-\tfrac{\gamma}{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(A(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1+\gamma}\Lambda_{R}^{1-\gamma}}}\right)^{j-1}B(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1+\tfrac{\gamma}{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R,1}}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(n^{(-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}+(-1+\gamma)(j-1))(1-t)}\tilde{O}(\sqrt{\log\tfrac{R}{\delta}}n^{(j-1)(\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}-\frac{(1+2\tau+2\alpha)t}{2\alpha}-\gamma(1-t))})\sqrt{\log\tfrac{R}{\delta}}n^{\frac{1}{2}}\|\bm{\mu}_{R,1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq n^{(-1+\frac{\gamma}{2})(1-t)+\frac{1}{2}-t}\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{[1-\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau)t](j-1)}{2\alpha}})\sqrt{\log\tfrac{R}{\delta}}\|\bm{\mu}_{R,1}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\gamma}{2}(1-t)+\frac{[1-\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau)t](j-1)}{2\alpha}}).

Since
\frac{1}{1-t}(\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}-\frac{(1+2\tau+2\alpha)t}{2\alpha})<\gamma<1
and

-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{1}{1-t}(\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}-\frac{(1+2\tau+2\alpha)t}{2\alpha})\frac{1-t}{2}<0
, we can let
\gamma
be a little bit larger than
\frac{1}{1-t}(\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}-\frac{(1+2\tau+2\alpha)t}{2\alpha})
and make
-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\gamma}{2}(1-t)<0
holds.
By (
67
), (
68
), (
69
), we have

\displaystyle\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R,1}\|_{2}

(70)

\displaystyle\leq O(1)+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\tilde{O}(n^{-\frac{1}{2}+\frac{\gamma}{2}(1-t)+\frac{[1-\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau)t](j-1)}{2\alpha}})

\displaystyle\leq O(1)+o(1)=O(1).

According to (
66
), we have
\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}\|_{2}=\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{-(1-t),\frac{(1-t)(1-2\beta)}{2\alpha}\}})+O(1)=O(1)
.
By Corollary
20
, with probability of at least
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\|\Phi_{R}(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}\|_{2}=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}(\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}\|_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n}}}\right).

From (
65
), we get
\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n}}}\right)
.
This concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma 31
.

Assume that
\sigma^{2}=\Theta(1)
. Let
R=n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa}
where
0<\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{\alpha^{2}}
. Assume that
\mu_{0}=0
. Then when
n
is sufficiently large, with probability of at least
1-3\delta
we have

\|(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}=\tilde{O}(\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\cdot n^{\max\{-1,\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}).

(71)

Assume that
\mu_{0}>0
. Then when
n
is sufficiently large, with probability of at least
1-3\delta
we have

\|(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}=\tilde{O}(\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n}).

(72)

Proof of Lemma
31
.

We have

\displaystyle(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})

(73)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}-(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}}}\right)f_{R}(\mathbf{x}).

When
\mu_{0}=0
, by Lemma
29
, with probability of at least
1-2\delta
, we have

\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}=\tilde{O}(\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\cdot n^{\max\{-1,\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}).

Since
\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{\alpha^{2}}<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{\alpha(1+2\tau)}
, we apply Lemma
28
and Corollary
26
and get that with probability of at least
1-\delta
, the second term in the right hand side of (
73
) is estimated as follows:

\displaystyle\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}-(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}}}\right)f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\|\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\tfrac{\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}}}}\right)^{j}(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\tfrac{\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}}}}\right)}}\right\|_{2}^{j}\|(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\tilde{O}(n^{-j\kappa\alpha})\tilde{O}(\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\cdot n^{\max\{-1,\tfrac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}})

\displaystyle=o(\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\cdot n^{\max\{-1,\tfrac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}).

Overall, from (
73
), we have that with probability
1-3\delta
,

\|(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}=\tilde{O}(\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\cdot n^{\max\{-1,\tfrac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}).

When
\mu_{0}>0
, using the same approach and Lemma
30
, we can prove that
\|(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}=\tilde{O}(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n})
.
This concludes the proof.
∎

Appendix D
Proof of the main results

D.1
Proofs related to the asymptotics of the normalized stochastic complexity

Lemma 32
.

Under Assumptions
4
,
5
and
6
, with probability of at least
1-2\delta
we have, we have

\displaystyle|T_{1,R}(D_{n})-T_{1}(D_{n})|=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(nR^{1-\alpha}+n^{1/2}R^{1-\alpha+\tau}+R^{1-\alpha+2\tau})}}\right)

(74)

If
R=n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa}
where
\kappa>0
, we have
|T_{1,R}(D_{n})-T_{1}(D_{n})|=o\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}}}\right)
.
If we further assume that
0<\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{\alpha^{2}}
,
\mu_{0}=0
and
\sigma^{2}=\Theta(1)
, then for sufficiently large
n

with probability of at least
1-4\delta
we have

|T_{2,R}(D_{n})-T_{2}(D_{n})|=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{\max\{(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\frac{1-2\beta}{2},1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+\frac{(1-2\beta)\kappa}{2},-1-\kappa\alpha,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}-\kappa\alpha\}}}}\right).

(75)

Proof of Lemma
32
.

Define
\Phi_{>R}=(\phi_{R+1}(\mathbf{x}),\phi_{R+2}(\mathbf{x}),\ldots,\phi_{p}(\mathbf{x}),\ldots)
, and
\Lambda_{>R}=\mathrm{diag}(\lambda_{R+1},\ldots,\lambda_{p},\ldots)
. We then have

\displaystyle|T_{1}(D_{n})-T_{1,R}(D_{n})|

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\frac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T})-\frac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T})}}\right|

(76)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\mathrm{Tr}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}-\mathrm{Tr}(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}}}\right|.

As for the first term in the right hand side of (
76
), we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\frac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T})-\frac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T})}}\right|

(77)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\frac{1}{2}\log\det\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T})^{-1}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T})}}\right)}}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\frac{1}{2}\log\det\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}}\right)}}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\tr\log\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}}\right)}}\right|.

Given a concave function
h
and a matrix
B\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times n}
whose eigenvalues
\zeta_{1},\ldots,\zeta_{n}
are all positive, we have that

\displaystyle\tr h(B)=\textstyle\sum_{p=1}^{n}h(\zeta_{i})\leq nh(\tfrac{1}{n}\textstyle\sum_{p=1}^{n}\zeta_{i})\leq nh(\tfrac{1}{n}\tr B),

(78)

where we used Jensen’s inequality. Using
h(x)=\log(1+x)
in (
78
), with probability
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\tfrac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T})-\tfrac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T})}}\right|

(79)

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{n}{2}\log(1+\tfrac{1}{n}\tr(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}))

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{n}{2}\log(1+\tfrac{1}{n\sigma^{2}}\|(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\|_{2}\tr(\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}))

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{n}{2}\log(1+\tfrac{1}{n\sigma^{2}}\textstyle\sum_{p=R+1}^{\infty}\lambda_{p}\|\phi_{p}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}^{2})\leq\tfrac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\textstyle\sum_{p=R+1}^{\infty}\lambda_{p}\|\phi_{p}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\textstyle\sum_{p=R+1}^{\infty}\lambda_{p}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(C_{\phi}^{2}\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\sqrt{p^{2\tau}n\|\phi_{p}\|_{2}^{2}}+p^{2\tau}}}\right)+n\|\phi_{p}\|_{2}^{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}n\textstyle\sum_{p=R+1}^{\infty}\lambda_{p}+n^{1/2}\textstyle\sum_{p=R+1}^{\infty}\lambda_{p}p^{\tau}+\textstyle\sum_{p=R+1}^{\infty}\lambda_{p}p^{2\tau})

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(nR^{1-\alpha}+n^{1/2}R^{1-\alpha+\tau}+R^{1-\alpha+2\tau})}}\right)=o\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}n^{\tfrac{1}{\alpha}}}}\right),

where in the second inequality we use the fact that
\tr AB\leq\|A\|_{2}\tr B
when
A
and
B
are symmetric positive definite matrices, and in the last inequality we use Lemma
18
.

As for the second term in the right hand side of (
76
), let
A=(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1/2}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\mathrm{Tr}(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}-\mathrm{Tr}(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}}}\right|

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\mathrm{Tr}A\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[I-(I+A(\frac{\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})A)^{-1}}}\right]A}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{1}{2}\tr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[I-(I+A(\tfrac{\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})A)^{-1}}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{n}{2}(1-(1+\tfrac{1}{n}\tr A(\tfrac{\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})A)^{-1})\leq\tfrac{n}{2}(1-(1+\tfrac{1}{n}\tr(\tfrac{\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}}))^{-1})

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{n}{2}(1-(1+\tfrac{1}{n\sigma^{2}}\textstyle\sum_{p=R+1}^{\infty}\lambda_{p}\|\phi_{p}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}^{2}))^{-1})\leq\tfrac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\textstyle\sum_{p=R+1}^{\infty}\lambda_{p}\|\phi_{p}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(nR^{1-\alpha}+n^{1/2}R^{1-\alpha+\tau}+R^{1-\alpha+2\tau})}}\right)=o\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}n^{\tfrac{1}{\alpha}}}}\right),

where in the first inequality we use the fact that
\|A\|_{2}<1
and
\tr ABA\leq\|A\|^{2}_{2}\tr B
when
A
and
B
are symmetric positive definite matrices, in the second inequality we use
h(x)=1-1/(1+x)
in (
78
) and in the last equality we use the last few steps of (
79
). This concludes the proof of the first statement.

As for
|T_{2}(D_{n})-T_{2,R}(D_{n})|
, we have

\displaystyle|T_{2}(D_{n})-T_{2,R}(D_{n})|

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f(\mathbf{x})-f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|

(80)

\displaystyle+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})-f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|.

For the first term on the right-hand side of (
80
), we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f(\mathbf{x})-f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}\|(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}+\|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}\|(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\|_{2}\|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}\|(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}+\|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})\|^{2}_{2}.

Applying Corollary
19
and Lemma
31
, with probability of at least
1-4\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f(\mathbf{x})-f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq 2\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)nR^{1-2\beta}}}}\right)\tilde{O}(\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\cdot n^{\max\{-1,\tfrac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}})+\tilde{O}((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)nR^{1-2\beta})

\displaystyle=2\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{1+(\tfrac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\tfrac{1-2\beta}{2}+\max\{-1,\tfrac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}}}\right)+\tilde{O}((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{1+(\tfrac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)(1-2\beta)})

\displaystyle=2\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{1+(\tfrac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\tfrac{1-2\beta}{2}+\max\{-1,\tfrac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}}}\right),

where the last equality holds because
(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\frac{1-2\beta}{2}<\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}
when
\kappa>0
.

As for the second term on the right-hand side of (
80
), according to Lemma
28
, Corollary
26
and Lemma
29
, we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\tfrac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})-f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|

(81)

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\tfrac{\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}}}}\right)^{j}(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\|(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\|_{2}^{j-1}\cdot\|\frac{\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}}\|_{2}^{j}\cdot\|(I+\tfrac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\tilde{O}(n^{-j\kappa\alpha})\tilde{O}((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{1+\max\{-2,\tfrac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}})

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{1+\max\{-2,\tfrac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}-\kappa\alpha}).

By (
80
), we have

\displaystyle|T_{2}(D_{n})-T_{2,R}(D_{n})|

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{1+(\tfrac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\tfrac{1-2\beta}{2}+\max\{-1,\tfrac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}}}\right)+\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{1+\max\{-2,\tfrac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}-\kappa\alpha}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{\max\{(\tfrac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\frac{1-2\beta}{2},1+\tfrac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+\tfrac{(1-2\beta)\kappa}{2},-1-\kappa\alpha,1+\tfrac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}-\kappa\alpha\}}}}\right).

This concludes the proof of the second statement.
∎

In Lemma
32
, we gave a bound for
|T_{2,R}(D_{n})-T_{2}(D_{n})|
when
n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}<R<n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}+\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{\alpha^{2}}}
. For
R>n
, we note the following lemma:

Lemma 33
.

Let
R=n^{C}
and
\sigma^{2}=n^{t}
. Assume that
C\geq 1
and
C(1-\alpha+2\tau)-t<0
. Under Assumptions
4
,
5
and
6
,
for sufficiently large
n
and with probability of at least
1-3\delta
we have

|T_{2,R}(D_{n})-T_{2}(D_{n})|=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}nR^{\max\{1/2-\beta,1-\alpha+2\tau\}}}}\right).

(82)

Proof of Lemma
33
.

Define
\Phi_{>R}=(\phi_{R+1}(\mathbf{x}),\phi_{R+2}(\mathbf{x}),\ldots,\phi_{p}(\mathbf{x}),\ldots)
, and
\Lambda_{>R}=\mathrm{diag}(\lambda_{R+1},\ldots,\lambda_{p},\ldots)
. Then we have

\displaystyle|T_{2}(D_{n})-T_{2,R}(D_{n})|

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f(\mathbf{x})-f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|

(83)

\displaystyle+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})-f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|.

For the first term on the right-hand side of (
83
), with probability
1-3\delta
we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f(\mathbf{x})-f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}\|(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\|_{2}\|f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}+\|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}\|(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\|_{2}\|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}\|f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}+\|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)nR^{1-2\beta}}}}\right)\tilde{O}(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\cdot\|f\|_{2})+\tilde{O}((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)nR^{1-2\beta})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)nR^{1/2-\beta}}}\right),

where we used Corollary
19
and Lemma
17
for the last inequality.

The assumption
C(1-\alpha+2\tau)-t<0
means that
\frac{R^{1-\alpha+2\tau}}{\sigma^{2}}=o(1)
. For the second term on the right-hand side of (
83
), by Lemmas
28
and
25
, we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})-f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|

(84)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\frac{\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}}}}\right)^{j}(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\|(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\|_{2}^{j+1}\cdot\|\frac{\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}}\|_{2}^{j}\cdot\|f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\tilde{O}(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}R^{j(1-\alpha+2\tau)})\tilde{O}((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n\|f\|_{2}^{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}nR^{1-\alpha+2\tau}).

Using (
83
), we have

\displaystyle|T_{2}(D_{n})-T_{2,R}(D_{n})|

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)nR^{1/2-\beta}}}\right)+\tilde{O}((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}R^{1-\alpha+2\tau})

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}R^{\max\{1/2-\beta,1-\alpha+2\tau\}}}}\right).

∎

Next we consider the asympototics of
T_{1,R}(D_{n})
and
T_{2,R}(D_{n})
.

Lemma 34
.

Let
A=(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-\gamma/2}\Lambda_{R}^{\gamma/2}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)\Lambda_{R}^{\gamma/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-\gamma/2}
. Assume that
\|A\|_{2}<1
where
\frac{1+2\tau}{\alpha}<\gamma\leq 1
. Then we have

\displaystyle T_{2,R}

\displaystyle(D_{n})=\tfrac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}+\tfrac{1}{2}\textstyle\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j+1}E_{j},

where

E_{j}=\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)}}\right)^{j-1}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}.

Proof of Lemma
34
.

Let
\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}=\mathrm{diag}\{\epsilon,\lambda_{1},\ldots,\lambda_{R}\}
. Since
\Lambda_{R}=\mathrm{diag}\{0,\lambda_{1},\ldots,\lambda_{R}\}
, we have that when
\epsilon
is sufficiently small,
\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R})^{-1/2}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}^{1/2}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R})^{-1/2}\|_{2}<1
. Since all diagonal entries of
\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}
are positive,
we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{R}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}\Phi_{R}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{R}\bm{\mu}_{R}

(85)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}^{T}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[I-\Phi_{R}(\sigma^{2}I+\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}\Phi^{T}_{R}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}}\right]\Phi_{R}\bm{\mu}_{R}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}\bm{\mu}_{R}-\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}(\sigma^{2}I+\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}\bm{\mu}_{R}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}(\sigma^{2}I+\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}^{-1}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}(\sigma^{2}I+\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}^{-1}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}.

Using Lemma
27
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}^{-1}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}

(86)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}^{-1}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j+1}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}^{-1}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R})^{-1}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)}}\right)^{j}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j+1}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R})^{-1}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R})^{-1}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)}}\right)^{j-1}

\displaystyle(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}

Letting
\epsilon\to 0
, we get

\displaystyle T_{2,R}(D_{n})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{R}\bm{\mu}_{R}

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\bigg[(-1)^{j+1}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)}}\right)^{j-1}

\displaystyle(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}\bigg]

This concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma 35
.

Assume that
\sigma^{2}=\Theta(1)
. Let
R=n^{\tfrac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa}
where
0<\kappa<\tfrac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{2\alpha^{2}}
.
Under Assumptions
4
,
5
and
6
, with probability of at least
1-\delta
, we have

T_{1,R}(D_{n})=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})-\tfrac{1}{2}\tr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I-(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}}}\right)}}\right)(1+o(1))=\Theta(n^{\tfrac{1}{\alpha}}).

(87)

Furthermore, if we assume
\mu_{0}=0
, we have

T_{2,R}(D_{n})=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}}}\right)(1+o(1))=\begin{cases}\Theta(n^{\max\{0,1+\tfrac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}),&\alpha\not=2\beta-1,\\
\Theta(\log n),&\alpha=2\beta-1.\end{cases}

(88)

Proof of Lemma
35
.

Let

A=(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-\gamma/2}\Lambda_{R}^{\gamma/2}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)\Lambda_{R}^{\gamma/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-\gamma/2},

(89)

where
\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}<\gamma\leq 1
. By Corollary
22
, with probability of at least
1-\delta
, we have

\|A\|_{2}=\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{1-2\gamma\alpha+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}}).

(90)

When
n
is sufficiently large,
\|A\|_{2}
is less than
1
. Let
B=(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}
. Then
\|B\|_{2}=\frac{\sigma^{2(1-\gamma)}}{n^{1-\gamma}}\|A\|_{2}=\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{1-\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}})
.
Using the Woodbury matrix identity, we compute
T_{1,R}(D_{n})
as follows:

\displaystyle T_{1,R}(D_{n})

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathrm{Tr}\Phi_{R}(\sigma^{2}I+\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}

(91)

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})+\tfrac{1}{2}\log\det[I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}]

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathrm{Tr}(\sigma^{2}I+\Lambda\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\Lambda\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})+\tfrac{1}{2}\tr\log[I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}B]-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathrm{Tr}(I-\sigma^{2}(\sigma^{2}I+\Lambda\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}))

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})+\tfrac{1}{2}\tr\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\tfrac{(-1)^{j-1}}{j}(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}B)^{j}

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathrm{Tr}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I-(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)}}\right)^{j}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathrm{Tr}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I-(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}}}\right)}}\right)+\tfrac{1}{2}\tr\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\tfrac{(-1)^{j-1}}{j}(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}B)^{j}

\displaystyle-\tfrac{1}{2}\mathrm{Tr}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}B^{j}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}}}\right),

where in the last equality we apply Lemma
27
.

Let
h(x)=\log(1+x)-(1-\frac{1}{1+x})
. It is easy to verify that
h(x)
is increasing on
[0,+\infty)
. As for the first term on the right hand side of (
91
), we have

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})-\tfrac{1}{2}\tr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I-(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{p=1}^{R}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\log(1+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\lambda_{p})-(1-\tfrac{1}{1+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\lambda_{p}})}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{p=1}^{R}h(\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\lambda_{p})\leq\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{p=1}^{R}h(\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\overline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}h(\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\overline{C_{\lambda}})+\tfrac{1}{2}\int_{[1,R]}h(\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\overline{C_{\lambda}}x^{-\alpha})\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}h(\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\overline{C_{\lambda}})+\tfrac{1}{2}n^{1/\alpha}\int_{[1/n^{1/\alpha},R/n^{1/\alpha}]}h(\tfrac{\overline{C_{\lambda}}}{\sigma^{2}}x^{-\alpha})\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Theta(n^{1/\alpha}),

where in the last equality we use the fact that
\int_{[0,+\infty]}h(x^{-\alpha})\mathrm{d}x<\infty
.
On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})-\tfrac{1}{2}\tr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I-(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{p=1}^{R}h(\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\lambda_{p})\geq\tfrac{1}{2}\sum_{p=1}^{R}h(\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\underline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha})

\displaystyle\geq

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\int_{[1,R+1]}h(\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\underline{C_{\lambda}}x^{-\alpha})\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}n^{1/\alpha}\int_{[1/n^{1/\alpha},(R+1)/n^{1/\alpha}]}h(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\underline{C_{\lambda}}x^{-\alpha})\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Theta(n^{1/\alpha}).

Overall, we have
\frac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})-\frac{1}{2}\tr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I-(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}}}\right)=\Theta(n^{1/\alpha})
.

As for the second term on the right hand side of (
91
), we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\tr\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\tfrac{(-1)^{j-1}}{j}(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}B)^{j}}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq R\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\|\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}B\|_{2}^{j}

\displaystyle=R\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{j(1-\alpha+2\tau)}{2\alpha}})

\displaystyle=R\tilde{O}(n^{\tfrac{1-\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}})=\tilde{O}(n^{\tfrac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa+\frac{1-\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}}).

As for the third term on the right hand side of (
91
), we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\mathrm{Tr}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}B^{j}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}}}\right)}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\mathrm{Tr}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}B^{j}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}}}\right)}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle R\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}B^{j}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle R\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}B^{j}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle R\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}B^{j}}}\right\|_{2}=\tilde{O}(n^{\tfrac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa+\tfrac{1-\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}}).

Then the asymptotics of
T_{1,R}(D_{n})
is given by

\displaystyle T_{1,R}(D_{n})=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})-\tfrac{1}{2}\tr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I-(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}}}\right)+\tilde{O}(n^{\tfrac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa+\tfrac{1-\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}})+\tilde{O}(n^{\tfrac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa+\tfrac{1-\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Theta(n^{1/\alpha})+\tilde{O}(n^{\tfrac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa+\tfrac{1-\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Theta(n^{\tfrac{1}{\alpha}}),

where in the last inequality we use the assumption that
\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{2\alpha}
. Since
\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa+\frac{1-\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}})
is lower order term compared to
\Theta(n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}})
, we further have

T_{1,R}(D_{n})=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})-\tfrac{1}{2}\tr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I-(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}}}\right)}}\right)(1+o(1)).

This concludes the proof of the first statement.

Let
\Lambda_{1:R}=\mathrm{diag}\{\lambda_{1},\ldots,\lambda_{R}\}
,
\Phi_{1:R}=(\phi_{1}(\mathbf{x}),\phi_{1}(\mathbf{x}),\ldots,\phi_{R}(\mathbf{x}))
and
\bm{\mu}_{1:R}=(\mu_{1},\ldots,\mu_{R})
. Since
\mu_{0}=0
, we have
T_{2,R}(D_{n})=\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{1:R}\Lambda_{1:R}\Phi_{1:R}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{1:R}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}
.
According to Lemma
34
, we have

\displaystyle T_{2,R}(D_{n})

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}

(92)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j+1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}^{T}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}(\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R}-nI)\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\Lambda_{1:R}(\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R}-nI)}}\right)^{j-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\bigg[(-1)^{j+1}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1+\gamma/2}\Lambda_{1:R}^{-\gamma/2}A\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1+\gamma}\Lambda_{1:R}^{1-\gamma}A}}\right)^{j-1}

\displaystyle(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1+\gamma/2}\Lambda_{1:R}^{-\gamma/2}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\bigg]

where in the second to last equality we used the definition of
A
(
89
). As for the first term on the right hand side of (
92
), by Lemma
15
, Assumption
4
and Assumption
5
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\leq\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\sum_{p=1}^{R}\frac{C_{\mu}^{2}p^{-2\beta}}{1+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\underline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}}=\begin{cases}\Theta(n^{\max\{0,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}),&\alpha\not=2\beta-1,\\
\Theta(\log n),&\alpha=2\beta-1.\end{cases}

On the other hand, by Assumption
5
, assuming that
\sup_{i\geq 1}p_{i+1}-p_{i}=h
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{\lfloor\frac{R}{h}\rfloor}\frac{\underline{C_{\mu}^{2}}p_{i}^{-2\beta}}{1+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\overline{C_{\lambda}}p_{i}^{-\alpha}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{\lfloor\frac{R}{h}\rfloor}\frac{\underline{C_{\mu}^{2}}i^{-2\beta}}{1+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\overline{C_{\lambda}}(hi)^{-\alpha}}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\Theta(n^{\max\{0,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}),&\alpha\not=2\beta-1,\\
\Theta(\log n),&\alpha=2\beta-1.\end{cases}

Overall, we have

\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}=\Theta(n^{\max\{0,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}\log^{k}n),

where
k=\begin{cases}0,&\alpha\not=2\beta-1,\\
1,&\alpha=2\beta-1.\end{cases}

By Lemma
16
, we have

\displaystyle\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1+\gamma/2}\Lambda_{1:R}^{-\gamma/2}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2}^{2}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{R}\frac{C_{\mu}^{2}p^{-2\beta}(\underline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha})^{-\gamma}}{(1+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\underline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha})^{2-\gamma}}

(93)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}(\max\{n^{-2+\gamma},R^{1-2\beta+\alpha\gamma}\})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{-2+\gamma,\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+\gamma+\kappa(1-2\beta+\alpha\gamma)\}}).

Using (
90
), the second term on the right hand side of (
92
) is computed as follows:

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\bigg[(-1)^{j+1}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1+\gamma/2}\Lambda_{1:R}^{-\gamma/2}A\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1+\gamma}\Lambda_{1:R}^{1-\gamma}A}}\right)^{j-1}

(94)

\displaystyle(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1+\gamma/2}\Lambda_{1:R}^{-\gamma/2}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\bigg]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\|A\|^{j}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}}}\right)^{(-1+\gamma)(j-1)}\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1+\gamma/2}\Lambda_{1:R}^{-\gamma/2}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{j(1-2\gamma\alpha+\alpha+2\tau)}{2\alpha}})\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}}}\right)^{(-1+\gamma)(j-1)}\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{-2+\gamma,\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+\gamma+\kappa(1-2\beta+\alpha\gamma)\}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{-2+\gamma+\frac{1-2\gamma\alpha+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha},\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+\gamma+\frac{1-2\gamma\alpha+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}+\kappa(1-2\beta+\alpha\gamma)\}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{-2+\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha},\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}+\kappa(1-2\beta+\alpha\gamma)\}}).

Since
\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}<\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{\alpha+1+2\tau}=1
, we have
-2+\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}<0
.Also we have

\displaystyle

\displaystyle\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}+\kappa(1-2\beta+\alpha\gamma)

(95)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+1+\frac{1-\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}+\kappa(1-2\beta+\alpha\gamma)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+1+\frac{1-\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}+\kappa\alpha\gamma

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+1,

where the last inequality holds because
\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{2\alpha^{2}}
and
\gamma\leq 1
. Hence we have

\displaystyle T_{2,R}(D_{n})=

\displaystyle\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}+\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{-2+\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha},\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}+\kappa(1-2\beta+\alpha\gamma)\}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Theta(n^{\max\{0,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}\log^{k}n)+\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{-2+\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha},\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}+\kappa(1-2\beta+\alpha\gamma)\}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Theta(n^{\max\{0,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}\log^{k}n).

where
k=\begin{cases}0,&\alpha\not=2\beta-1,\\
1,&\alpha=2\beta-1.\end{cases}
. Since
\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{-2+\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha},\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}+\kappa(1-2\beta+\alpha\gamma)\}})
is lower order term compared to
\Theta(n^{\max\{0,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}\log^{k}n)
, we further have

\displaystyle T_{2,R}(D_{n})=

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}}}\right)(1+o(1))

This concludes the proof of the second statement.
∎

Lemma 36
.

Under Assumptions
4
,
5
and
6
, with probability of at least
1-5\delta
, we have

T_{1}(D_{n})=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)-\frac{1}{2}\tr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I-(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1}}}\right)}}\right)(1+o(1))=\Theta(n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}),

(96)

Furthermore, let
\delta=n^{-q}
where
0\leq q<\min\{\frac{(2\beta-1)(\alpha-1-2\tau)}{4\alpha^{2}},\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{2\alpha}\}
. If we assume
\mu_{0}=0
, we have

T_{2}(D_{n})=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1}\bm{\mu}}}\right)(1+o(1))=\begin{cases}\Theta(n^{\max\{0,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}),&\alpha\not=2\beta-1,\\
\Theta(\log n),&\alpha=2\beta-1.\end{cases}

(97)

Proof of Lemma
36
.

Let
R=n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa}
where
0\leq\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{2\alpha^{2}}
. By Lemmas
32
and
35
, with probability of at least
1-5\delta
we have

|T_{1,R}(D_{n})-T_{1}(D_{n})|=\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa(1-\alpha)}),

(98)

and

|T_{2,R}(D_{n})-T_{2}(D_{n})|=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{\max\{(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\frac{1-2\beta}{2},1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+\frac{(1-2\beta)\kappa}{2},-1-\kappa\alpha,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}-\kappa\alpha\}}}}\right)

(99)

as well as

T_{1,R}(D_{n})=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})-\frac{1}{2}\tr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I-(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}}}\right)}}\right)(1+o(1))=\Theta(n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}),

(100)

and

T_{2,R}(D_{n})=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1}\bm{\mu}}}\right)(1+o(1))=\begin{cases}\Theta(n^{\max\{0,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}),&\alpha\not=2\beta-1,\\
\Theta(\log n),&\alpha=2\beta-1.\end{cases}

(101)

We then have

\displaystyle T_{1}(D_{n})

\displaystyle=T_{1,R}(D_{n})+T_{1,R}(D_{n})-T_{1}(D_{n})=\Theta(n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}})+\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa(1-\alpha)})=\Theta(n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}).

Since
\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa(1-\alpha)})
is lower order term compared to
\Theta(n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}})
, we further have

\displaystyle T_{1}(D_{n})=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})-\frac{1}{2}\tr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I-(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}}}\right)}}\right)(1+o(1))=\Theta(n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}})

Besides, we have

\displaystyle\log\det(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)-\log\det(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})

\displaystyle=\sum_{p=R+1}^{\infty}\log(1+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\lambda_{p})\leq\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\sum_{p=R+1}^{\infty}\lambda_{p}\leq\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\sum_{p=R+1}^{\infty}C_{\lambda}p^{-\alpha}=\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}O(R^{1-\alpha})

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}O(n^{(1-\alpha)(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)})

\displaystyle=o(n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}).

Then we have
\log\det(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})=\log\det(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)(1+o(1))
. Similarly we can prove
\tr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I-(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1}}}\right)=\tr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I-(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}}}\right)(1+o(1))
.
This concludes the proof of the first statement.

As for
T_{2}(D_{n})
, we have

\displaystyle T_{2}(D_{n})

\displaystyle=T_{2,R}(D_{n})+T_{2,R}(D_{n})-T_{2}(D_{n})

\displaystyle=\Theta(n^{\max\{0,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}\log^{k}n)+\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{\max\{(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\frac{1-2\beta}{2},1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+\frac{(1-2\beta)\kappa}{2},-1-\kappa\alpha,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}-\kappa\alpha\}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\Theta(n^{\max\{0,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}\log^{k}n)+\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(n^{q+\max\{(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\frac{1-2\beta}{2},1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+\frac{(1-2\beta)\kappa}{2},-1-\kappa\alpha,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}-\kappa\alpha\}}}}\right)

where we use
\delta=n^{-q}
,
k=\begin{cases}0,&\alpha\not=2\beta-1,\\
1,&\alpha=2\beta-1.\end{cases}
.

Since
0\leq\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{2\alpha^{2}}
and
0\leq q<\min\{\frac{(2\beta-1)(\alpha-1-2\tau)}{4\alpha^{2}},\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{2\alpha}\}
, we can choose
\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{2\alpha^{2}}
and
\kappa
is arbitrarily close to
\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{2\alpha^{2}}
such that
0\leq q<\min\{\frac{(2\beta-1)\kappa}{2},\kappa\alpha\}
. Then we have
(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\frac{1-2\beta}{2}+q<0
,
-1-\kappa\alpha+q<0
,
\frac{(1-2\beta)\kappa}{2}+q<0
and
-\kappa\alpha+q<0
. So we have

\displaystyle T_{2,R}(D_{n})=\Theta(n^{\max\{0,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}\log^{k}n).

Since
\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{\max\{(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\frac{1-2\beta}{2},1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+\frac{(1-2\beta)\kappa}{2},-1-\kappa\alpha,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}-\kappa\alpha\}}}}\right)
is lower order term compared to
\Theta(n^{\max\{0,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}\log^{k}n)
, we further have

T_{2}(D_{n})=T_{2,R}(D_{n})(1+o(1))=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}}}\right)(1+o(1)).

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\bm{\mu}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1}\bm{\mu}-\bm{\mu_{R}}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu_{R}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{p=R+1}^{\infty}\frac{\mu_{p}^{2}}{(1+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\lambda_{p})}\leq\sum_{p=R+1}^{\infty}\mu_{p}^{2}\leq\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\sum_{p=R+1}^{\infty}C_{\mu}^{2}p^{-2\beta}=O(R^{1-2\beta})

\displaystyle=O(n^{(1-2\beta)(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)})

\displaystyle=o(n^{\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}}).

Then we have
\bm{\mu}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1}\bm{\mu}=\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}(1+o(1))
.
This concludes the proof of the second statement.
∎

Proof of Theorem
7
.

Using Lemma
36
and noting that
\frac{1}{\alpha}>0
, with probability of at least
1-5\tilde{\delta}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}F^{0}(D_{n})

\displaystyle=T_{1}(D_{n})+T_{2}(D_{n})

\displaystyle=\bigg[\frac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})-\frac{1}{2}\tr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I-(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}\bigg](1+o(1))

\displaystyle=\Theta(n^{\max\{{\frac{1}{\alpha},\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+1\}}})

Letting
\delta=5\tilde{\delta}
, we get the result.
∎

In the case of
\mu_{0}>0
, we have the following lemma:

Lemma 37
.

Assume that
\sigma^{2}=\Theta(1)
. Let
R=n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa}
where
0<\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{\alpha^{2}}
. Assume that
\mu_{0}>0
. Under Assumptions
4
,
5
and
6
, for sufficiently large
n

with probability of at least
1-4\delta
we have

|T_{2,R}(D_{n})-T_{2}(D_{n})|=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{\max\{1+(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\frac{1-2\beta}{2},1-\kappa\alpha\}}}}\right)..

(102)

Proof of Lemma
37
.

As for
|T_{2}(D_{n})-T_{2,R}(D_{n})|
, we have

\displaystyle|T_{2}(D_{n})-T_{2,R}(D_{n})|

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f(\mathbf{x})-f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|

(103)

\displaystyle+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})-f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|.

For the first term on the right-hand side of (
103
), we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f(\mathbf{x})-f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}\|(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}+\|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}\|(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\|_{2}\|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}\|(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}+\|f_{>R}(\mathbf{x})\|^{2}_{2}.

Applying Corollary
19
and Lemma
31
, with probability of at least
1-4\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f(\mathbf{x})-f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)nR^{1-2\beta}}}}\right)\tilde{O}(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n})+\tilde{O}((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)nR^{1-2\beta})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{1+(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\frac{1-2\beta}{2}}}}\right)+\tilde{O}((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{1+(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)(1-2\beta)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{1+(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\frac{1-2\beta}{2}}}}\right).

As for the second term on the right-hand side of (
80
), according to Lemma
28
, Corollary
26
and Lemma
30
, we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})-f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|

(104)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})^{T}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\frac{\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}}}}\right)^{j}(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\|(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\|_{2}^{j-1}\cdot\|\frac{\Phi_{>R}\Lambda_{>R}\Phi_{>R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}}\|_{2}^{j}\cdot\|(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}f_{R}(\mathbf{x})\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\tilde{O}(n^{-j\kappa\alpha})\tilde{O}((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{1-\kappa\alpha}).

By (
80
), we have

\displaystyle|T_{2}(D_{n})-T_{2,R}(D_{n})|

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{1+(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\frac{1-2\beta}{2}}}}\right)+\tilde{O}((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{1-\kappa\alpha})

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{\max\{1+(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\frac{1-2\beta}{2},1-\kappa\alpha\}}}}\right).

∎

Lemma 38
.

Assume that
\sigma^{2}=\Theta(1)
. Let
R=n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa}
where
0<\kappa<\min\{\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{2\alpha^{2}},\frac{2\beta-1}{\alpha^{2}}\}
. Assume that
\mu_{0}>0
.
Under Assumptions
4
,
5
and
6
, with probability of at least
1-\delta
, we have

T_{2,R}(D_{n})=\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\mu_{0}^{2}+\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{\frac{1+7\alpha+2\tau}{8\alpha},1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}).

(105)

Proof of Lemma
38
.

Let

A=(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-\gamma/2}\Lambda_{R}^{\gamma/2}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)\Lambda_{R}^{\gamma/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-\gamma/2},

(106)

where
\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}<\gamma\leq 1
. By Corollary
22
, with probability of at least
1-\delta
, we have

\|A\|_{2}=\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{1-2\gamma\alpha+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}}).

(107)

When
n
is sufficiently large,
\|A\|_{2}
is less than
1
. Let
\bm{\mu}_{R,1}=(\mu_{0},0,\ldots,0)
and
\bm{\mu}_{R,2}=(0,\mu_{1},\ldots,\mu_{R})
. Then
\bm{\mu}_{R}=\bm{\mu}_{R,1}+\bm{\mu}_{R,2}
. Let
\tilde{\Lambda}_{1,R}=\mathrm{diag}\{1,\lambda_{1},\ldots,\lambda_{R}\}
and
I_{0,R}=(0,1,\ldots,1)
.
Then
\Lambda_{R}=\tilde{\Lambda}_{1,R}I_{0,R}
. Let
B=(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\tilde{\Lambda}_{1,R}^{1/2}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)\tilde{\Lambda}_{1,R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}
. By Corollary
23
, we have
\|B\|_{2}=O(\sqrt{\log\frac{R}{\delta}}n^{\frac{1}{2}})
. By Lemma
34
, we have

\displaystyle T_{2,R}(D_{n})

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}

(108)

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\bigg[(-1)^{j+1}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)}}\right)^{j-1}

\displaystyle(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}\bigg]

As for the first term on the right hand side of (
108
), by Lemma
15
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\mu^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1}\mu\leq\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mu_{0}^{2}+\sum_{p=1}^{R}\frac{C_{\mu}^{2}p^{-2\beta}}{1+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\underline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}}}}\right)=\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\mu_{0}^{2}+\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{0,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}).

We define
Q_{1,j}
,
Q_{2,j}
and
Q_{3,j}
by

\displaystyle Q_{1,j}

\displaystyle=\bm{\mu}_{R,1}^{T}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)}}\right)^{j-1}

(109)

\displaystyle(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R,1}

\displaystyle Q_{2,j}

\displaystyle=\bm{\mu}_{R,1}^{T}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)}}\right)^{j-1}

\displaystyle(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R,2}

\displaystyle Q_{3,j}

\displaystyle=\bm{\mu}_{R,2}^{T}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)}}\right)^{j-1}

\displaystyle(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R,2}

The quantity
Q_{3,j}
actually shows up in the case of
\mu_{0}=0
in the proof of Lemma
35
. By (
92
), (
94
) and (
95
), we have that

|\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j+1}Q_{3,j}|=|\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j+1}\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{(j-1)(1-\alpha+2\tau)}{2\alpha}})o(n^{\max\{0,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}})|=o(n^{\max\{0,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}).

(110)

For
Q_{1,j}
, we have

\displaystyle Q_{1,1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\bm{\mu}_{R,1}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}}B(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R,1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\|\bm{\mu}_{R,1}\|_{2}^{2}\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}}\|_{2}^{2}\|B\|_{2}

\displaystyle=O(\sqrt{\log\frac{R}{\delta}}n^{\frac{1}{2}}),

where in the last equality we use
\|B\|_{2}=O(\sqrt{\log\frac{R}{\delta}}n^{\frac{1}{2}})
. For
j\geq 2
, we have

\displaystyle Q_{1,j}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\bm{\mu}_{R,1}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}}B\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1+\gamma}\Lambda_{R}^{1-\gamma}A}}\right)^{j-2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1+\gamma}\Lambda_{R}^{1-\gamma}

\displaystyle B(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R,1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\|\bm{\mu}_{R,1}\|_{2}^{2}\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}}\|_{2}^{2}\|B\|^{2}_{2}\|A\|^{j-2}_{2}\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1+\gamma}\Lambda_{R}^{1-\gamma}\|_{2}^{j-1}

\displaystyle=O(\log\frac{R}{\delta}n\cdot n^{\frac{(j-2)(1-2\gamma\alpha+\alpha+2\tau)}{2\alpha}}\cdot n^{-(1-\gamma)(j-1)})

\displaystyle=O(\log\frac{R}{\delta}n^{\gamma}\cdot n^{\frac{(j-2)(1-\alpha+2\tau)}{2\alpha}}).

Then we have

|\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j+1}Q_{1,j}|\leq O(\sqrt{\log\frac{R}{\delta}}n^{\frac{1}{2}})+\sum_{j=2}^{\infty}O(\log\frac{R}{\delta}n^{\gamma}\cdot n^{\frac{(j-2)(1-\alpha+2\tau)}{2\alpha}})=O(\log\frac{R}{\delta}n^{\gamma})

(111)

For
Q_{2,j}
, we have

\displaystyle Q_{2,j}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\bm{\mu}_{R,1}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}}B\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1+\gamma}\Lambda_{R}^{1-\gamma}A}}\right)^{j-1}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}}\tilde{\Lambda}_{1,R}^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R,2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\|\bm{\mu}_{R,1}\|_{2}\|B\|_{2}\|A\|^{j-1}_{2}\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1+\gamma}\Lambda_{R}^{1-\gamma}\|_{2}^{j-1}\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}}\tilde{\Lambda}_{1,R}^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R,2}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=O(\sqrt{\log\frac{R}{\delta}}n^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot n^{\frac{(j-1)(1-\alpha+2\tau)}{2\alpha}})\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}}\tilde{\Lambda}_{1,R}^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R,2}\|_{2}.

Since
\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}}\tilde{\Lambda}_{1,R}^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R,2}\|_{2}
is actually the case of
\mu_{0}=0
, we can use (
93
) in the proof of Lemma
35
and get

\displaystyle\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}}\tilde{\Lambda}_{1,R}^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R,2}\|_{2}^{2}=

\displaystyle\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1+\gamma/2}\Lambda_{1:R}^{-\gamma/2}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2}^{2}

(112)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{-2+\gamma,\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+\gamma+\kappa(1-2\beta+\alpha\gamma)\}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{-2+\gamma,\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}+\gamma+\kappa(1-2\beta+\alpha\gamma)\}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle o(n^{\gamma}),

where in the last equality we use
\kappa<\frac{2\beta-1}{\alpha^{2}}
.
Then we have

|\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j+1}Q_{2,j}|\leq\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}o(\sqrt{\log\frac{R}{\delta}}n^{\frac{1+\gamma}{2}}\cdot n^{\frac{(j-1)(1-\alpha+2\tau)}{2\alpha}})=o(\sqrt{\log\frac{R}{\delta}}n^{\frac{1+\gamma}{2}})

(113)

Choosing
\gamma=\frac{1}{2}(1+\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha})=\frac{1+3\alpha+2\tau}{4\alpha}<1
, we have

\displaystyle T_{2,R}(D_{n})

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j+1}(Q_{1,j}+Q_{2,j}+Q_{3,j})

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\mu_{0}^{2}+\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{0,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}})+o(n^{\max\{0,1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}})+O(\log\frac{R}{\delta}n^{\gamma})+o(\sqrt{\log\frac{R}{\delta}}n^{\frac{1+\gamma}{2}})

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\mu_{0}^{2}+\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{\frac{1+\gamma}{2},1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}})

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\mu_{0}^{2}+\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{\frac{1+7\alpha+2\tau}{8\alpha},1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}).

∎

Proof of Theorem
8
.

Let
R=n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa}
where
0<\kappa<\min\{\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{2\alpha^{2}},\frac{2\beta-1}{\alpha^{2}}\}
. Since
0\leq q<\min\{\frac{2\beta-1}{2},\alpha\}\cdot\min\{\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{2\alpha^{2}},\frac{2\beta-1}{\alpha^{2}}\}
, we can choose
\kappa<\min\{\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{2\alpha^{2}},\frac{2\beta-1}{\alpha^{2}}\}
and
\kappa
is arbitrarily close to
\kappa<\min\{\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau}{2\alpha^{2}},\frac{2\beta-1}{\alpha^{2}}\}
such that
0\leq q<\min\{\frac{(2\beta-1)\kappa}{2},\kappa\alpha\}
. Then we have
(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\frac{1-2\beta}{2}+q<0
, and
-\kappa\alpha+q<0
.
As for
T_{2}(D_{n})
, we have

\displaystyle T_{2}(D_{n})

\displaystyle\leq T_{2,R}(D_{n})+|T_{2,R}(D_{n})-T_{2}(D_{n})|

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\mu_{0}^{2}+\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{\frac{1+7\alpha+2\tau}{8\alpha},1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}})+\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{\max\{1+(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\frac{1-2\beta}{2},1-\kappa\alpha\}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\mu_{0}^{2}+\tilde{O}(n^{\max\{\frac{1+7\alpha+2\tau}{8\alpha},1+\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}})+\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(n^{q+\max\{1+(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)\frac{1-2\beta}{2},1-\kappa\alpha\}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\mu_{0}^{2}+o(n).

By Lemma
36
, we have
T_{1}(D_{n})=O(n^{\frac{1}{\alpha}})
.
Hence
\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}F^{0}(D_{n})=T_{1}(D_{n})+T_{2}(D_{n})=\frac{n}{2\sigma^{2}}\mu_{0}^{2}+o(n)
.
∎

D.2
Proofs related to the asymptotics of the generalization error

Lemma 39
.

Assume
\sigma^{2}=\Theta(n^{t})
where
1-\frac{\alpha}{1+2\tau}<t<1
. Let
R=n^{(\frac{2\alpha-1}{\alpha(\alpha-1)}+1)(1-t)}
. Under Assumptions
4
,
5
and
6
, with probability of at least
1-\delta
over sample inputs
(x_{i})_{i=1}^{n}
, we have

G_{1}(D_{n})=\tfrac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tr(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}-\|\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\|^{2}_{F}}}\right)=\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Theta\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(n^{\tfrac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}}}}\right).

(114)

Proof of Lemma
39
.

Let
G_{1,R}(D_{n})=\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}(T_{1,R}(D_{n+1})-T_{1,R}(D_{n}))
, where
R=n^{C}
for some constant C.
By Lemma
32
,
we have that

\displaystyle|G_{1}(D_{n})-G_{1,R}(D_{n})|

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}[T_{1}(D_{n+1})-T_{1,R}(D_{n+1})]-[T_{1}(D_{n})-T_{1,R}(D_{n})]}}\right|

(115)

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}O((n+1)R^{1-\alpha})}}\right|+\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|O(nR^{1-\alpha})]}}\right|

\displaystyle=O(\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}nR^{1-\alpha}).

Define
\eta_{R}=(\phi_{0}(x_{n+1}),\phi_{1}(x_{n+1}),\ldots,\phi_{R}(x_{n+1}))^{T}
and
\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}=(\Phi_{R}^{T},\eta_{R})^{T}
. As for
G_{1,R}(D_{n})
, we have

\displaystyle G_{1,R}(D_{n})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}(T_{1,R}(D_{n+1})-T_{1,R}(D_{n}))

(116)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\frac{\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}\Lambda_{R}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})-\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{Tr}(I-(I+\frac{\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}\Lambda_{R}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1})}}\right)

\displaystyle-\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{2}\log\det(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi^{T}_{R}}{\sigma^{2}})-\frac{1}{2}\mathrm{Tr}(I-(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi^{T}_{R}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1})}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\log\det(I+\frac{\widetilde{\Phi_{R}}\Lambda_{R}\widetilde{\Phi_{R}}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})-\log\det(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})}}\right)

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\mathrm{Tr}(I-(I+\frac{\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}\Lambda_{R}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1})-\mathrm{Tr}(I-(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1})}}\right).

As for the first term in the right hand side (
116
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\log\det(I+\frac{\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}\Lambda_{R}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})-\log\det(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\log\det(I+\frac{\Lambda_{R}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}^{T}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}}{\sigma^{2}})-\log\det(I+\frac{\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}}{\sigma^{2}})}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\log\det(I+\frac{\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}+\eta_{R}\eta_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})-\log\det(I+\frac{\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}}{\sigma^{2}})}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\log\det\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((I+\frac{\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}(I+\frac{\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}}{\sigma^{2}}+\frac{\Lambda_{R}\eta_{R}\eta_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})}}\right)}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\log\det\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I+(I+\frac{\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\frac{\Lambda_{R}\eta_{R}\eta_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}}}}\right)}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\log\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(1+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\eta_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\eta_{R}}}\right)}}\right)

Let
