A=(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}.

(117)

According to Corollary
22
, with probability of at least
1-\delta
, we have
\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}=O(\sqrt{\log\frac{R}{\delta}}n^{\frac{1-\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}-\frac{(1+2\tau)t}{2\alpha}})=o(1)
.
When
n
is sufficiently large,
\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}
is less than
1
.
By Lemma
27
, we have

\displaystyle\eta_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\eta_{R}

(118)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\eta_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\eta_{R}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\eta_{R}^{T}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)}}\right)^{j}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\eta_{R}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\eta_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\eta_{R}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\eta_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2j}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}A^{j}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}\eta_{R}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\eta_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\eta_{R}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}^{j}\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}\eta_{R}\|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{R}\phi^{2}_{p}(x_{n+1})\frac{\overline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}}{1+n\underline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}/\sigma^{2}}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}^{j}\sum_{p=1}^{R}\phi^{2}_{p}(x_{n+1})\frac{\overline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}}{1+n\underline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}/\sigma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1}^{R}\frac{\overline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}p^{2\tau}}{1+n\underline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}/\sigma^{2}}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}^{j}\sum_{p=1}^{R}\frac{\overline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}p^{2\tau}}{1+n\underline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}/\sigma^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle O(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha+2\tau)(1-t)}{\alpha}})+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}^{j}O(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha+2\tau)(1-t)}{\alpha}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle O(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha+2\tau)(1-t)}{\alpha}})=o(1),

where we use Lemma
15
in the last inequality. Next we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\log\det(I+\frac{\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}\Lambda_{R}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})-\log\det(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\log\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(1+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\eta_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\eta_{R}}}\right)}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\eta_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{\Lambda_{R}\Phi^{T}_{R}\Phi_{R}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\eta_{R}}}\right)(1+o(1))}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tr(I+\frac{\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}}}\right)(1+o(1)),

where in the last equality we use the fact that
\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\eta_{R}\eta_{R}^{T}=I
. By Lemma
27
, we have

\displaystyle\tr(I+\frac{\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tr(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\tr\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}(\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}-nI)}}\right)^{j}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tr(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\tr\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}A^{j}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}.

By Lemma
15
, we have

\displaystyle\tr(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{p=1}^{R}\frac{\overline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}}{1+n\underline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}/\sigma^{2}}=\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})

\displaystyle\tr(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{p=1}^{R}\frac{\underline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}}{1+n\overline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}/\sigma^{2}}=\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}}).

Overall,

\tr(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}=\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}}).

(119)

Since
\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|^{j}_{2}=o(1)
, we have that the absolute values of diagonal entries of
\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}A^{j}
are at most
o(1)
. Let
(A^{j})_{p,p}
denote the
(p,p)
-th entry of the matrix
A^{j}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\tr\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}A^{j}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}}}\right|

(120)

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\sum_{p=1}^{R}\frac{\lambda_{p}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}(A^{j})_{p,p}}{1+n\lambda_{p}/\sigma^{2}}}}\right|\leq\sum_{p=1}^{R}\frac{\lambda_{p}\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}A\|^{j}_{2}}{1+n\lambda_{p}/\sigma^{2}}=\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{j(1-\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau)t)}{2\alpha}}(\log R)^{j/2}),

where in the last step we used (
119
).
According to (
119
) and (
120
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\log\det(I+\frac{\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}\Lambda_{R}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})-\log\det(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})}}\right)

(121)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tr(I+\frac{\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}}}\right)(1+o(1))=\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{j(1-\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau)t)}{2\alpha}}(\log R)^{j/2})

\displaystyle=\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})o(1)=\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tr(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}}}\right)(1+o(1)).

Using the Woodbury matrix identity, the second term in the right hand side (
116
) is given by

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\mathrm{Tr}(I-(I+\frac{\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}\Lambda_{R}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}-\mathrm{Tr}(I-(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\tr(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}^{T}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}^{T}-\tr(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{R}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\tr(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}^{T}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}^{T}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}-\tr(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}}}\right)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\tr(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}^{T}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R})^{-1}-\tr(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\tr(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\eta_{R}\eta_{R}^{T})^{-1}-\tr(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\tr\frac{(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\eta_{R}\eta_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}}{1+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\eta_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\eta_{R}}}}\right),

where the last equality uses the Sherman–Morrison formula. According to (
118
), we get

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\tr\frac{(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\eta_{R}\eta_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}}{1+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\eta_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\eta_{R}}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\tr(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}\eta_{R}\eta_{R}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}(1+o(1))}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\tr(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\tr\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\tr(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\tr(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2})^{-1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\|\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\|\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A)^{-1}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\|_{F}^{2},

where in the penultimate equality we use
\tr(BB^{T})=\|B\|_{F}^{2}
,
\|B\|_{F}
is the Frobenius norm of
A
, and in the last equality we use the definition of
A
(
117
). Then we have

\displaystyle\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\|\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A)^{-1}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\|_{F}^{2}

(122)

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\|\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}(I+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}A^{j})(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\|\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}A^{j}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\|_{F}^{2}.

By Lemma
15
, we have

\displaystyle\|\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\|_{F}\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{p=1}^{R}\frac{\overline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}}{(1+n\underline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}/\sigma^{2})^{2}}}=\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{2\alpha}})

\displaystyle\|\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\|_{F}\geq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{p=1}^{R}\frac{\underline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}}{(1+n\overline{C_{\lambda}}p^{-\alpha}/\sigma^{2})^{2}}}=\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{2\alpha}}).

Overall, we have

\|\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\|_{F}=\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{2\alpha}}).

(123)

Since
\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}=O(\sqrt{\log\frac{R}{\delta}}n^{\frac{1-\alpha+2\tau}{2\alpha}-\frac{(1+2\tau)t}{2\alpha}})=o(1)
, we have

\displaystyle\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}A^{j}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\|_{F}

(124)

\displaystyle\leq\|\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\|_{F}\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}^{j}\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=O(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{2\alpha}})\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{j(1-\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau)t)}{2\alpha}}(\log R)^{j/2}),

where in the first inequality we use the fact that
\|AB\|_{F}\leq\|A\|_{F}\|B\|_{2}
when
B
is symmetric.
By Lemma
15
, we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\tr\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}A^{j}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}}}\right|

(125)

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\sum_{p=1}^{R}\frac{\lambda_{p}((\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A)^{j})_{p,p}}{(1+n\lambda_{p}/\sigma^{2})^{2}}}}\right|\leq\sum_{p=1}^{R}\frac{\lambda_{p}\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|^{j}_{2}}{(1+n\lambda_{p}/\sigma^{2})^{2}}=\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{j(1-\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau)t)}{2\alpha}}(\log R)^{j/2}),

According to (
123
), (
124
) and (
125
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\mathrm{Tr}(I-(I+\frac{\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}\Lambda_{R}\widetilde{\Phi}_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}-\mathrm{Tr}(I-(I+\frac{\Phi_{R}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}}{\sigma^{2}})^{-1}}}\right)

(126)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\tr(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R}\Phi_{R}^{T}\Phi_{R})^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\|\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}A^{j}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\bigg(\|\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}A^{j}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}}}\right\|_{F}^{2}

\displaystyle+2\tr\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}A^{j}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1/2}\bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\bigg(\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}O(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{j(1-\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau)t)}{2\alpha}}(\log R)^{j/2})

\displaystyle+2\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{j(1-\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau)t)}{2\alpha}}(\log R)^{j/2})\bigg)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})=\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\|\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}.

Combining (
121
) and (
126
) we get that
G_{1,R}(D_{n})=\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}(\tr(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}+\|\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\|^{2}_{F})=\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})
.
From (
115
) we have that
G_{1}(D_{n})\leq G_{1,R}(D_{n})+|G_{1}(D_{n})-G_{1,R}(D_{n})|=\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})+O(n\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}R^{1-\alpha})
.
Choosing
R=n^{(\frac{2\alpha-1}{\alpha(\alpha-1)}+1)(1-t)}
we conclude the proof.
∎

Lemma 40
.

Assume
\sigma^{2}=\Theta(n^{t})
where
1-\frac{\alpha}{1+2\tau}<t<1
. Let
S=n^{D}
. Assume that
\|\xi\|_{2}=1
. When
n
is sufficiently large, with probability of at least
1-2\delta
we have

\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S}\xi\|_{2}=O(\sqrt{(\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\cdot n^{-(1-t)}).

(127)

Proof of Lemma
40
.

Using the Woodbury matrix identity, we have that

\displaystyle((I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S}\xi=

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[I-\Phi_{S}(\sigma^{2}I+\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}}}\right]\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S}\xi

(128)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S}\xi-\Phi_{S}(\sigma^{2}I+\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S}\xi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Phi_{S}(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}\xi.

Let
A=(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-\gamma/2}\Lambda_{S}^{\gamma/2}(\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S}-nI)\Lambda_{S}^{\gamma/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-\gamma/2}
, where
\gamma>\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau+2\alpha)t}{2\alpha(1-t)}
. By Corollary
22
, with probability of at least
1-\delta
, we have
\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}=\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau+2\alpha)t}{2\alpha}-\gamma(1-t)})
.
When
n
is sufficiently large,
\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}
is less than
1
.
By Lemma
27
, we have

\displaystyle(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}(\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S}-nI)}}\right)^{j}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1}.

Then we have

\displaystyle\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}\xi\|_{2}

(129)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}(-1)^{j}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}(\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S}-nI)}}\right)^{j}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1}}}\right)\Lambda_{S}\xi}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}\xi\|_{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}(\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S}-nI)}}\right)^{j}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}\xi}}\right\|_{2}}}\right).

For the first term in the right hand side of the last equation, we have

\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}\xi\|_{2}\leq\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}\|_{2}\|\xi\|_{2}\leq\frac{\sigma^{2}}{n}=O(n^{-(1-t)}).

(130)

Using the fact that
\|\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}A\|_{2}=\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{1+\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau+2\alpha)t}{2\alpha}-\gamma(1-t)})
and
\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}\|_{2}\leq n^{-1}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}(\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S}-nI)}}\right)^{j}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}\xi}}\right\|_{2}

(131)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sigma^{2j}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}}\Lambda_{S}^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(A(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1+\gamma}\Lambda_{S}^{1-\gamma}}}\right)^{j-1}A(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}}\Lambda_{S}^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\Lambda_{S}\xi}}\right\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle n^{(1-t)(-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}+(-1+\gamma)(j-1))}\tilde{O}(n^{\frac{j(1+\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau+2\alpha)t)}{2\alpha}-j\gamma(1-t)})\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}}\Lambda_{S}^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\xi\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}(n^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}(1-t)+\frac{(1-\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau)t)j}{2\alpha}})\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S})^{-1+\frac{\gamma}{2}}\Lambda_{S}^{1-\frac{\gamma}{2}}\|_{2}\|\xi\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}(n^{-\frac{\gamma}{2}(1-t)+\frac{(1-\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau)t)j}{2\alpha}})O(n^{(-1+\gamma/2)(1-t)})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}(n^{-(1-t)+\frac{(1-\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau)t)j}{2\alpha}}).

Using (
129
), (
130
) and (
131
), we have

\displaystyle\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}\xi\|_{2}

(132)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tilde{O}(n^{-(1-t)})+\sum_{j=1}^{\infty}\tilde{O}(n^{-1+\frac{(1-\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau)t)j}{2\alpha}})}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tilde{O}(n^{-(1-t)})+\tilde{O}(n^{-1+\frac{1-\alpha+2\tau-(1+2\tau)t}{2\alpha}})}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}(n^{-(1-t)}).

By Corollary
20
, with probability of at least
1-\delta
, we have

\displaystyle\|\Phi_{S}(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}\xi\|_{2}=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}\xi\|_{2})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tilde{O}(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\cdot n^{-(1-t)}).

From (
128
) we get
\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T})^{-1}f_{S}(\mathbf{x})\|_{2}=\tilde{O}(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\cdot n^{-(1-t)})
.
This concludes the proof.
∎

Lemma 41
.

Assume
\sigma^{2}=\Theta(n^{t})
where
1-\frac{\alpha}{1+2\tau}<t<1
. Let
\delta=n^{-q}
where
0\leq q<\frac{[\alpha-(1+2\tau)(1-t)](2\beta-1)}{4\alpha^{2}}
. Under Assumptions
4
,
5
and
6
, assume that
\mu_{0}=0
. Let
R=n^{(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)(1-t)}
where
0<\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau+(1+2\tau)t}{2\alpha^{2}(1-t)}
. Then with probability of at least
1-6\delta
over sample inputs
(x_{i})_{i=1}^{n}
, we have

G_{2}(D_{n})=\frac{(1+o(1))}{2\sigma^{2}}\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}\|_{2}^{2}=\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Theta(n^{\max\{-2(1-t),\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)
, where
k=\begin{cases}0,&2\alpha\not=2\beta-1,\\
1,&2\alpha=2\beta-1.\end{cases}
.

Proof of Lemma
41
.

Let
S=n^{D}
. Let
G_{2,S}(D_{n})=\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}(T_{2,S}(D_{n+1})-T_{2,S}(D_{n}))
.
By Lemma
33
, when
S>n^{\max\{1,\frac{-t}{(\alpha-1-2\tau)}\}}
with probability of at least
1-3\delta
we have that

\displaystyle|G_{2}(D_{n})-G_{2,S}(D_{n})|=|\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}[T_{2}(D_{n+1})-T_{2,S}(D_{n+1})]-[T_{2}(D_{n})-T_{2,S}(D_{n})]|

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(n+1)S^{\max\{1/2-\beta,1-\alpha+2\tau\}}}}\right)-\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}nS^{\max\{1/2-\beta,1-\alpha+2\tau\}}}}\right)}}\right|

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}nS^{\max\{1/2-\beta,1-\alpha+2\tau\}}}}\right)

(133)

Let
\Lambda_{1:S}=\mathrm{diag}\{\lambda_{1},\ldots,\lambda_{S}\}
,
\Phi_{1:S}=(\phi_{1}(\mathbf{x}),\phi_{1}(\mathbf{x}),\ldots,\phi_{S}(\mathbf{x}))
and
\bm{\mu}_{1:S}=(\mu_{1},\ldots,\mu_{S})
. Since
\mu_{0}=0
, we have
T_{2,S}(D_{n})=\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{1:S}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{1:S}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}
.
Define
\eta_{1:S}=(\phi_{1}(x_{n+1}),\ldots,\phi_{S}(x_{n+1}))^{T}
and
\widetilde{\Phi}_{1:S}=(\Phi_{1:S}^{T},\eta_{1:S})^{T}
.
In the proof of Lemma
34
, we showed that

\displaystyle T_{2,S}(D_{n})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{1:S}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{1:S}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}^{T}\Lambda_{1:S}^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}^{T}\Lambda_{1:S}^{-1}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}.

We have

\displaystyle G_{2,S}(D_{n})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}(T_{2,S}(D_{{n+1}})-T_{2,S}(D_{n}))

(135)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}^{T}\Lambda_{1:S}^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}^{T}\Lambda_{1:S}^{-1}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\widetilde{\Phi}_{S}^{T}\widetilde{\Phi}_{S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}}}\right)

\displaystyle-\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}^{T}\Lambda_{1:S}^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}^{T}\Lambda_{1:S}^{-1}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:S})}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}^{T}\Lambda_{1:S}^{-1}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\frac{1}{2}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}^{T}\Lambda_{1:S}^{-1}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\widetilde{\Phi}_{S}^{T}\widetilde{\Phi}_{S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}^{T}\Lambda_{1:S}^{-1}\frac{(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\Lambda_{1:S}\eta_{1:S}\eta_{1:S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}}{1+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\eta_{1:S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\Lambda_{1:S}\eta_{1:S}}\bm{\mu}_{1:S})}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\frac{\bm{\mu}_{1:S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S}\Lambda_{1:S})^{-1}\eta_{1:S}\eta_{1:S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}}{1+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\eta_{1:S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\Lambda_{1:S}\eta_{1:S}})}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S}\Lambda_{1:S})^{-1}\eta_{1:S}\eta_{1:S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S}\Lambda_{1:S})^{-1}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}\|_{2}^{2},

where in the fourth to last equality we used the Sherman–Morrison formula, in the third inequality we used (
118
)
, and in the last equality we used the fact that
\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\eta_{1:S}\eta_{1:S}^{T}=I
.

Let
\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R}=(\mu_{1},\ldots,\mu_{R},0,\ldots,0)\in\mathbb{R}^{S}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R}\|_{2}+\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}(\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R})\|_{2},

(136)

\displaystyle\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R}\|_{2}-\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}(\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R})\|_{2}.

Let
R=n^{(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)(1-t)}
where
0<\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau+(1+2\tau)t}{2\alpha^{2}(1-t)}
.
In Lemma
29
, (
62
), we showed that with probability of at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R}\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta(n^{(1-t)\max\{-1,\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)

(137)

\displaystyle=(1+o(1))\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2},

where
k=\begin{cases}0,&2\alpha\not=2\beta-1,\\
1,&2\alpha=2\beta-1.\end{cases}
.
The same proof holds if we replace
\Phi_{1:R}
with
\Phi_{1:S}
,
\Lambda_{1:R}
with
\Lambda_{1:S}
, and
\bm{\mu}_{1:R}
with
\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R}
. We have

\displaystyle\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta(n^{(1-t)\max\{-1,\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)

(138)

\displaystyle=(1+o(1))\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S})^{-1}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R}\|_{2}.

Next we bound
\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}(\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R})\|_{2}
. By Assumption
5
, we have that
\|\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R}\|_{2}=O(R^{\frac{1-2\beta}{2}})
. For any
\xi\in\mathbb{R}^{S}
and
\|\xi\|_{2}=1
, using the Woodbury matrix identity, with probability of at least
1-2\delta
we have

\displaystyle|\xi^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}(\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R})|

\displaystyle=|\xi^{T}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{1:S}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{1:S}}}\right)(\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R})|

\displaystyle=|\xi^{T}(\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R})-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\xi^{T}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{1:S}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{1:S}(\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R})|

\displaystyle\leq\|\xi\|_{2}\|\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}|\xi^{T}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{1:S}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{1:S}(\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R})|

\displaystyle\leq O(R^{\frac{1-2\beta}{2}})+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{1:S}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{1:S}\Lambda_{1:S}\xi\|_{2}\|\Phi_{1:S}(\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R})\|_{2}

\displaystyle=O(R^{\frac{1-2\beta}{2}})+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}O(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\cdot n^{-(1-t)})O(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n}R^{\frac{1-2\beta}{2}})

\displaystyle=O((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)R^{\frac{1-2\beta}{2}}),

where in the second to last step we used Corollary
20
to show
\|\Phi_{1:S}(\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R})\|_{2}=O(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n}R^{\frac{1-2\beta}{2}})
with probability of at least
1-\delta
, and Lemma
40
to show that
\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{1:S}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{1:S}\Lambda_{1:S}\xi\|_{2}=O(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\cdot n^{-1})
with probability of at least
1-\delta
. Since
R=n^{(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)(1-t)}
, we have

\displaystyle|\xi^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}(\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R})|=O((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{2\alpha}+\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)\kappa}{2}}).

Since
\xi
is arbitrary, we have
\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}(\bm{\mu}_{1:S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R})\|_{2}=O((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{2\alpha}+\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)\kappa}{2}})
.
Since
0\leq q<\frac{[\alpha-(1+2\tau)(1-t)](2\beta-1)}{4\alpha^{2}}
and
0<\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau+(1+2\tau)t}{2\alpha^{2}(1-t)}
, we can choose
\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau+(1+2\tau)t}{2\alpha^{2}(1-t)}
and
\kappa
is arbitrarily close to
\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau+(1+2\tau)t}{2\alpha^{2}(1-t)}
such that
0\leq q<\frac{(2\beta-1)(1-t)\kappa}{2}
. Then we have
\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)\kappa}{2}+q<0
.
From (
136
) and (
138
), we have

\displaystyle\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}\|_{2}=

\displaystyle\Theta(n^{\max\{-(1-t),\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{2\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)+O((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{2\alpha}+\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)\kappa}{2}})

(139)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Theta(n^{\max\{-(1-t),\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{2\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)+O((n^{q+\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{2\alpha}+\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)\kappa}{2}})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Theta(n^{\max\{-(1-t),\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{2\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1+o(1))\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S})^{-1}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle(1+o(1))\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}\|_{2}.

Hence
G_{2,S}(D_{n})=\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:S}\|_{2}^{2}=\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Theta(n^{(1-t)\max\{-2,\frac{1-2\beta}{\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)
.
Then by (
D.2
), we have

G_{2}(D_{n})=\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Theta(n^{\max\{-2(1-t),\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)+\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}S^{\max\{1/2-\beta,1-\alpha+2\tau\}}}}\right).

Choosing
S=n^{\max\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{1,\frac{-t}{(\alpha-1-2\tau)},\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1+q+\min\{2,\frac{2\beta-1}{\alpha}\}}{\min\{\beta-1/2,\alpha-1-2\tau\}}+1}}\right)(1-t)}}\right\}}
, we get the result.
∎

Proof of Theorem
9
.

From Lemmas
39
and
41
and
\frac{1}{\alpha}-1>-2
, we have that with probability of at least
1-7\tilde{\delta}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}G(D_{n})=

\displaystyle\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}(\tr(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}-\|\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\|^{2}_{F}+\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}\|^{2}_{2})

(140)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Theta(n^{\max\{-2(1-t),\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Theta(n^{\max\{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha},\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{\alpha}\}})

where
k=\begin{cases}0,&2\alpha\not=2\beta-1\\
1,&2\alpha=2\beta-1\end{cases}
, and
R=n^{(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)(1-t)}
,
\kappa>0
.

Furthermore, we have

\displaystyle\tr(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1}\Lambda-\tr(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}

\displaystyle=\sum_{p=R+1}^{\infty}\frac{\lambda_{p}}{1+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\lambda_{p}}\leq\sum_{p=R+1}^{\infty}\frac{C_{\lambda}p^{-\alpha}}{1+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}C_{\lambda}p^{-\alpha}}\leq\sum_{p=R+1}^{\infty}C_{\lambda}p^{-\alpha}=\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}O(R^{1-\alpha})

\displaystyle=O(n^{(1-\alpha)(1-t)(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)})

\displaystyle=o(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}}).

Then we have

\tr(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\Lambda_{R}=\tr(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1}\Lambda(1+o(1)).

(141)

Similarly we can prove

\displaystyle\|\Lambda_{R}^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\|^{2}_{F}

\displaystyle=\|\Lambda^{1/2}(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1}\|^{2}_{F}(1+o(1))

(142)

\displaystyle\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{R}\|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle=\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1}\bm{\mu}\|^{2}_{2}(1+o(1))

(143)

Letting
\delta=7\tilde{\delta}
, the proof is complete.
∎

In the case of
\mu_{0}>0
, we have the following lemma:

Lemma 42
.

Let
\delta=n^{-q}
where
0\leq q<\frac{[\alpha-(1+2\tau)(1-t)](2\beta-1)}{4\alpha^{2}}
.
Under Assumptions
4
,
5
and
6
, assume that
\mu_{0}>0
. Then with probability of at least
1-6\delta
over sample inputs
(x_{i})_{i=1}^{n}
, we have

G_{2}(D_{n})=\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\mu_{0}^{2}+o(1)
.

Proof of Lemma
42
.

Let
S=n^{D}
. Let
G_{2,S}(D_{n})=\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}(T_{2,S}(D_{n+1})-T_{2,S}(D_{n}))
.
By Lemma
33
, when
S>n^{\max\{1,\frac{-t}{(\alpha-1-2\tau)}\}}
, with probability of at least
1-3\delta
we have that

\displaystyle|G_{2}(D_{n})-G_{2,S}(D_{n})|=|\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}[T_{2}(D_{n+1})-T_{2,S}(D_{n+1})]-[T_{2}(D_{n})-T_{2,S}(D_{n})]|

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left|\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}(n+1)S^{\max\{1/2-\beta,1-\alpha+2\tau\}}}}\right)-\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}nS^{\max\{1/2-\beta,1-\alpha+2\tau\}}}}\right)}}\right|

\displaystyle=\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\tfrac{1}{\delta}+1)\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}nS^{\max\{1/2-\beta,1-\alpha+2\tau\}}}}\right)

Let
\Lambda_{S}=\mathrm{diag}\{\lambda_{1},\ldots,\lambda_{S}\}
,
\Phi_{S}=(\phi_{1}(\mathbf{x}),\phi_{1}(\mathbf{x}),\ldots,\phi_{S}(\mathbf{x}))
and
\bm{\mu}_{S}=(\mu_{1},\ldots,\mu_{S})
.
Define
\eta_{S}=(\phi_{0}(x_{n+1}),\phi_{1}(x_{n+1}),\ldots,\phi_{S}(x_{n+1}))^{T}
and
\widetilde{\Phi}_{S}=(\Phi_{S}^{T},\eta_{S})^{T}
.
By the same technique as in the proof of Lemma
34
, we replace
\Lambda_{R}
by
\tilde{\Lambda}_{\epsilon,R}=\mathrm{diag}\{\epsilon,\lambda_{1},\ldots,\lambda_{R}\}
, let
\epsilon\to 0
and show the counterpart of the result (
135
) in the proof of Lemma
41
:

\displaystyle G_{2,S}(D_{n})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}(T_{2,S}(D_{{n+1}})-T_{2,S}(D_{n}))

(144)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\frac{\bm{\mu}_{S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S})^{-1}\eta_{S}\eta_{S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{S}}{1+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\eta_{S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\Lambda_{S}\eta_{S}})}}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S})^{-1}\eta_{S}\eta_{S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{S}}}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\bm{\mu}_{S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S})^{-1}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{S}

\displaystyle=\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{S}\|_{2}^{2},

where in the fourth to last equality we used the Sherman–Morrison formula, in the third inequality we used (
118
)
, and in the last equality we used the fact that
\mathbb{E}_{(x_{n+1},y_{n+1})}\eta_{1:S}\eta_{1:S}^{T}=I
.

Let
\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R}=(\mu_{0},\mu_{1},\ldots,\mu_{R},0,\ldots,0)\in\mathbb{R}^{S}
. Then we have

\displaystyle\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{S}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R}\|_{2}+\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}(\bm{\mu}_{S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R})\|_{2},

(145)

\displaystyle\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{S}\|_{2}

\displaystyle\geq\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R}\|_{2}-\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}(\bm{\mu}_{S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R})\|_{2}.

Choose
R=n^{(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)(1-t)}
where
0<\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau+(1+2\tau)t}{\alpha^{2}(1-t)}
.
In Lemma
29
, (
62
), we showed that with probability of at least
1-\delta
,

\displaystyle\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R}\Phi_{1:R}^{T}\Phi_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta(n^{(1-t)\max\{-1,\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)

(146)

\displaystyle=(1+o(1))\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:R})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{1:R}\|_{2},

where
k=\begin{cases}0,&2\alpha\not=2\beta-1,\\
1,&2\alpha=2\beta-1.\end{cases}
.
The same proof holds if we replace
\Phi_{1:R}
with
\Phi_{1:S}
,
\Lambda_{1:R}
with
\Lambda_{1:S}
, and
\bm{\mu}_{1:R}
with
\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R}
. We have

\displaystyle\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S}\Phi_{1:S}^{T}\Phi_{1:S})^{-1}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Theta(n^{(1-t)\max\{-1,\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)

(147)

\displaystyle=(1+o(1))\|(I+\frac{n}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{1:S})^{-1}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{1:R}\|_{2}.

So we have

\displaystyle\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R}\|_{2}

\displaystyle=\mu_{0}+\Theta(n^{(1-t)\max\{-1,\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)

(148)

\displaystyle=\mu_{0}+o(1).

Next we bound
\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}(\bm{\mu}_{S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R})\|_{2}
. By Assumption
5
, we have that
\|\bm{\mu}_{S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R}\|_{2}=O(R^{\frac{1-2\beta}{2}})
. For any
\xi\in\mathbb{R}^{S}
and
\|\xi\|_{2}=1
, using the Woodbury matrix identity, with probability of at least
1-2\delta
we have

\displaystyle|\xi^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}(\bm{\mu}_{S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R})|

\displaystyle=|\xi^{T}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{S}}}\right)(\bm{\mu}_{S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R})|

\displaystyle=|\xi^{T}(\bm{\mu}_{S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R})-\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\xi^{T}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{S}(\bm{\mu}_{S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R})|

\displaystyle\leq\|\xi\|_{2}\|\bm{\mu}_{S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R}\|_{2}+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}|\xi^{T}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{S}(\bm{\mu}_{S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R})|

\displaystyle\leq O(R^{\frac{1-2\beta}{2}})+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S}\xi\|_{2}\|\Phi_{S}(\bm{\mu}_{S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R})\|_{2}

\displaystyle=O(R^{\frac{1-2\beta}{2}})+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}O(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\cdot n^{-(1-t)})O(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n}R^{\frac{1-2\beta}{2}})

\displaystyle=O((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)R^{\frac{1-2\beta}{2}}),

where in the second to last step we used Corollary
20
to show
\|\Phi_{S}(\bm{\mu}_{S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R})\|_{2}=O(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n}R^{\frac{1-2\beta}{2}})
with probability of at least
1-\delta
, and Lemma
40
to show that
\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T})^{-1}\Phi_{S}\Lambda_{S}\xi\|_{2}=O(\sqrt{(\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n}\cdot n^{-(1-t)})
with probability of at least
1-\delta
.
Since
R=n^{(\frac{1}{\alpha}+\kappa)(1-t)}
, we have

\displaystyle|\xi^{T}(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}(\bm{\mu}_{S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R})|=O((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{2\alpha}+\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)\kappa}{2}}).

Since
\xi
is arbitrary, we have
\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}(\bm{\mu}_{S}-\hat{\bm{\mu}}_{R})\|_{2}=O((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{2\alpha}+\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)\kappa}{2}})
.
Since
0\leq q<\frac{[\alpha-(1+2\tau)(1-t)](2\beta-1)}{4\alpha^{2}}
and
0<\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau+(1+2\tau)t}{2\alpha^{2}(1-t)}
, we can choose
\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau+(1+2\tau)t}{2\alpha^{2}(1-t)}
and
\kappa
is arbitrarily close to
\kappa<\frac{\alpha-1-2\tau+(1+2\tau)t}{2\alpha^{2}(1-t)}
such that
0\leq q<\frac{(2\beta-1)(1-t)\kappa}{2}
. Then we have
\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)\kappa}{2}+q<0
.
From (
145
) and (
148
), we have

\displaystyle\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{S}\|_{2}=

\displaystyle\mu_{0}+\Theta(n^{(1-t)\max\{-1,\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)+O((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)n^{\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{2\alpha}+\frac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)\kappa}{2}})

(149)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu_{0}+\Theta(n^{(1-t)\max\{-1,\frac{1-2\beta}{2\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu_{0}+o(1).

Hence
G_{2,S}(D_{n})=\frac{1+o(1)}{2\sigma^{2}}\|(I+\frac{1}{\sigma^{2}}\Lambda_{S}\Phi_{S}^{T}\Phi_{S})^{-1}\bm{\mu}_{S}\|_{2}^{2}=\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\mu_{0}^{2}+o(1)
.
Then by (
D.2
),
G_{2}(D_{n})=\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\mu_{0}^{2}+o(1)+\tilde{O}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left((\frac{1}{\delta}+1)nS^{\max\{1/2-\beta,1-\alpha\}}}}\right)
. Choosing
S=n^{\max\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left\{1,\frac{-t}{(\alpha-1-2\tau)},\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\frac{1+q+\min\{2,\frac{2\beta-1}{\alpha}\}}{\min\{\beta-1/2,\alpha-1-2\tau\}}+1}}\right)(1-t)}}\right\}}
, we get the result.
∎

Proof of Theorem
11
.

According to Lemma
42
,

G_{2}(D_{n})=\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\mu_{0}^{2}+o(1)
.
By Lemma
39
, we have
G_{1}(D_{n})=\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})
. Then
\mathbb{E}_{\epsilon}G(D_{n})=G_{1}(D_{n})+G_{2}(D_{n})=\frac{1}{2\sigma^{2}}\mu_{0}^{2}+o(1)
.
∎

D.3
Proofs related to the excess mean squared generalization error

Proof of Theorem
12
.

For
\mu_{0}=0
, we can show that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}M(D_{n})

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}\mathbb{E}_{x_{n+1}}[\bar{m}(x_{n+1})-f(x_{n+1})]^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}\mathbb{E}_{x_{n+1}}[K_{x_{n+1}\mathbf{x}}(K_{n}+\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}I_{n})^{-1}\mathbf{y}-f(x_{n+1})]^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}\mathbb{E}_{x_{n+1}}[\eta^{T}\Lambda\Phi^{T}[\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}+\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}I_{n})^{-1}(\Phi\mu+\bm{\epsilon})-\eta^{T}\mu]^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}\mathbb{E}_{x_{n+1}}[\eta^{T}\Lambda\Phi^{T}(\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}+\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}I_{n})^{-1}\bm{\epsilon}]^{2}

\displaystyle+\mathbb{E}_{x_{n+1}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left[\eta^{T}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\Lambda\Phi^{T}(\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}+\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}I_{n})^{-1}\Phi-I}}\right)\mu}}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=\sigma_{\mathrm{true}}^{2}\tr\Lambda\Phi^{T}(\Phi\Lambda\Phi^{T}+\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}I_{n})^{-2}\Phi\Lambda

\displaystyle+\mu^{T}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}}\Phi^{T}\Phi\Lambda}}\right)^{-1}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}}\Lambda\Phi^{T}\Phi}}\right)^{-1}\mu

\displaystyle=\tfrac{\sigma_{\mathrm{true}}^{2}}{\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}}\tr(I+\tfrac{\Lambda\Phi^{T}\Phi}{\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}})^{-1}\Lambda-\tr(I+\tfrac{\Lambda\Phi^{T}\Phi}{\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}})^{-2}\Lambda+\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}}\Lambda\Phi^{T}\Phi)^{-1}\mu\|_{2}^{2}.

According to (
139
) from the proof of Lemma
41
, the truncation procedure (
D.2
) and (
143
), with probability of at least
1-\delta
we have

\|(I+\tfrac{1}{\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}}\Lambda\Phi^{T}\Phi)^{-1}\mu\|_{2}^{2}=\Theta(n^{\max\{-2(1-t),\tfrac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)=(1+o(1))\|(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1}\bm{\mu}\|^{2}_{2},

where
k=\begin{cases}0,&2\alpha\not=2\beta-1,\\
1,&2\alpha=2\beta-1.\end{cases}
.

According to (
121
) and (
126
) from the proof of Lemma
39
, the truncation procedure (
115
), (
141
) and (
142
), with probability of at least
1-\delta
we have

\displaystyle\tr(I+\tfrac{\Lambda\Phi^{T}\Phi}{\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}})^{-1}\Lambda-\tr(I+\tfrac{\Lambda\Phi^{T}\Phi}{\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}})^{-2}\Lambda

\displaystyle=\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tr(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1}\Lambda}}\right)(1+o(1))-\|\Lambda^{1/2}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1}\|_{F}^{2}(1+o(1))

\displaystyle=\Theta(n^{\tfrac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}}).

Combining the above two equations we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}M(D_{n})=

\displaystyle(1+o(1))\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tfrac{\sigma_{\mathrm{true}}^{2}}{\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}}\mathopen{}\mathclose{{\left(\tr(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1}\Lambda-\|\Lambda^{1/2}(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1}\|^{2}_{F}}}\right)+\|(I+\tfrac{n}{\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}}\Lambda)^{-1}\bm{\mu}\|^{2}_{2}}}\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\tfrac{\sigma_{\mathrm{true}}^{2}}{\sigma_{\mathrm{model}}^{2}}\Theta(n^{\tfrac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})+\Theta(n^{\max\{-2(1-t),\tfrac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\displaystyle\sigma_{\mathrm{true}}^{2}}\Theta(n^{\tfrac{1-\alpha-t}{\alpha}})+\Theta(n^{\max\{-2(1-t),\tfrac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{\alpha}\}}\log^{k/2}n)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\Theta\bigg(\max\{\sigma_{\mathrm{true}}^{2}n^{\tfrac{1-\alpha-t}{\alpha}},n^{\tfrac{(1-2\beta)(1-t)}{\alpha}}\}\bigg)

When
\mu_{0}>0
, according to (
149
) in the proof of Lemma
42
and the truncation procedure (
D.2
), with probability of at least
1-\delta
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{\epsilon}}M(D_{n})=

\displaystyle\Theta(n^{\frac{(1-\alpha)(1-t)}{\alpha}})+\mu_{0}^{2}+o(1)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu_{0}^{2}+o(1).

∎
